- **ccpm**: Lean Project Management for solo developers managing multiple projects. 19 optimized commands, 50-60% token reduction via smart caching, natural workflow (plan, work, sync, commit, verify, done), tool-agnostic architecture supporting any PM system via MCP (Linear, Jira, Confluence, etc.), intelligent agent auto-invocation, interactive planning with deep research, monorepo support, safety-first design.
